  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学者对Mapudungun语前动词性词根的语法分类存在严重分歧——Smeets将其归为助动词，Longkon视为情态前缀，Zúñiga则认为是前动词助词或复合动词词干。这种分类混乱阻碍了对该语言语法系统的准确理解，亟需通过系统性语料证据加以澄清。

Method: 采用历时语料库分析，覆盖1606年至今约400年的语料，追踪各词根的V1（动词第一位）出现频率变化。通过对比稳定词、波动词和真正助词的不同演变轨迹，结合早期传教士正字法中空格所反映的韵律停顿的石化现象，提出韵律-正字法假说来解释表层的"可变绑定"现象。

Result: 三类不同演变轮廓的发现：kim和shinge呈现稳定的V1复合词行为；pepi和wuno的V1频率波动与正字法变迁相关；kalli被确认为真正的助词。此外，kim和kupa的V2位置语料证实其可作为完整词汇动词使用，支持激进并合模型。

Conclusion: 证据支持Zúñiga的激进并合（radical concatenation）作为正确的语法模型。早期传教士将韵律停顿转录为空格的做法被后人重新解读为句法边界，导致了表面上的"可变绑定"假象。该研究对没有前接触书面传统的语言研究具有方法论启示。

Abstract: This study examines the grammatical status of preverbal uninflected and underived roots in Mapudungun, with particular focus on wuno 'return/re-'. Through a critical review of scholarly classifications--auxiliaries (Smeets, 2008), modal prefixes (Longkon, 2011), and preverbal particles/complex verb stems (Zúñiga, 2006)--we demonstrate the limitations of existing frameworks. A diachronic corpus analysis spanning four centuries (1606-present) reveals that these elements exhibit three distinct profiles: stable V1 compounds (kim, shinge), volatile V1 rates reflecting orthographic shift (pepi, wuno), and a true particle (kalli). The discovery of V2 attestations for kim and kupa confirms their status as full lexical verbs. We propose a prosodic-orthographic hypothesis: apparent "variable binding" results from the fossilization of prosodic pauses transcribed by early missionaries as spaces, a convention later reanalysed by speakers as syntactic boundaries. The evidence supports Zúñiga's radical concatenation as the correct grammatical model, with implications for the study of languages with no pre-contact written tradition.

</details>


### [167] [A-SR: Self-Evolving Agentic LLMs for Symbolic Regression via Hierarchical Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04872)
*Wenxiao Zhao,Dong Liu,Kaiyi Xu,Feng Liu,Zhen Zhao,Fei Ben,Shu Wang,Wenhao Li,Yingnian Wu,Fenghua Ling,Haobo Li,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出A-SR，一个自进化的代理框架用于符号回归，将控制单元从表达式编辑转变为角色条件证据视图，通过协调协议路由、在线评估者-奖励角色策略和状态路由过程记忆来协调公式发现，在LLM-SRBench四个LSR-Synth科学领域上将Acc@0.01从25.79%提升到48.30%（Llama3.1-8B），并在真实世界科学发现任务中取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM引导的符号回归方法依赖统一的提议循环，将异构的搜索失败压缩为标量分数和单一提示，无法充分利用搜索过程中的多样化信息（如可靠性与生产率的差异、失败模式的不同类型），导致搜索效率受限。

Method: A-SR将搜索控制从表达式编辑转向角色条件证据视图，包含三个核心组件：(1) 协调协议间的路由；(2) 在线评估者-奖励角色策略，评估者反馈同时刻画可靠性和生产率，更新角色级效用；(3) 状态路由过程记忆，将精英母题、失败轨迹和有效性诊断路由到不同代理。框架在两个时间尺度上自进化：运行内适应搜索过程（不更新LLM参数），运行间将记录轨迹蒸馏为开源LLM的角色条件提议先验。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench四个LSR-Synth科学领域中，A-SR将Acc@0.01从基线25.79%提升至48.30%（Llama3.1-8B）；A-SR-LoRA将Qwen3-4B结果从24.58%提升至38.29%。在四个真实世界科学发现任务中，A-SR在8个报告指标中的7个上获得最优的分布内或分布外归一化均方误差。

Conclusion: A-SR通过角色条件证据视图和双时间尺度自进化机制，证明将异构搜索反馈按角色分流并动态路由可以显著提升LLM引导符号回归的性能，且蒸馏后的开源LLM在效率与效果上均优于基线。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to discover closed-form equations from data, but existing LLM-guided methods often rely on a unified proposal loop that compresses heterogeneous search failures into a scalar score and a single prompt. We propose A-SR, a self-evolving agentic framework that shifts the control unit from expression edits to role-conditioned evidence views. A-SR coordinates formula discovery through routing among coordination protocols, an online evaluator-reward role policy, and state-routed process memory. During search, evaluator feedback characterizes reliability and productivity, updates role-level utilities, and routes elite motifs, failure traces, and validity diagnostics to different agents. The framework self-evolves at two timescales: within a run, it adapts the search process without updating LLM parameters; across runs, recorded trajectories can be distilled into open-source LLMs as role-conditioned proposal priors. Averaged over the four LSR-Synth scientific domains in LLM-SRBench, A-SR improves Acc@0.01 over baselines from 25.79% to 48.30% with Llama3.1-8B, while A-SR-LoRA improves the corresponding Qwen3-4B result from 24.58% to 38.29%. On four real-world scientific discovery tasks, A-SR obtains the best in-distribution or out-of-distribution normalized mean squared error on 7 of 8 reported metrics.

</details>


### [168] [Evaluation Pitfalls and Sparsity Limitations in LLM-based Confidence Estimates for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04899)
*Elena Merdjanovska,Omar Zaidan,Andreas Rücklé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在分类任务中的置信度言语化（verbalization）输出极其稀疏（如Qwen3-32B在SST-2上仅输出8种独特置信度值），这种稀疏性不仅限制了实用性，还会严重影响AUARC评估指标的结果。作者主张标准化使用阶梯插值（stepwise interpolation）进行公平比较，并提出verbalization logprobs方法——利用token概率对言语化数字加权，在不增加推理成本的情况下解决了稀疏性问题并取得最佳AUARC性能（比原始verbalization高2.3个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于分类任务时，置信度估计至关重要，但常见的verbalization方法产生极其稀疏的输出，严重限制实际用途。更关键的是，这种稀疏性会扭曲评估结果——AUARC中插值方法的选择会戏剧性地改变模型排名（consistency sampling在阶梯插值下从最佳跌至最差）。因此需要公平且标准化的评估方式，并改进置信度估计方法。

Method: （1）系统性地揭示了verbalization输出的稀疏性现象，在4个数据集和2个LLM上进行了观察验证；（2）分析插值方法（linear vs. stepwise）对AUARC评估排名的影响；（3）提出verbalization logprobs方法：将verbalized数字按token概率加权，以解决稀疏性并提升置信度质量，且无需额外推理成本。

Result: （1）Qwen3-32B在SST-2上仅verbalize出8种唯一的置信度值，其中超过一半恰好为95%；（2）插值选择会改变模型排名（consistency sampling在两种插值下从最佳变为最差）；（3）在标准化阶梯插值下，verbalization logprobs达到最佳AUARC，比vanilla verbalization高出2.3个百分点，且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 建议将阶梯插值标准化为AUARC评估的默认方法，以保证公平比较；verbalization logprobs作为一种零额外成本的改进方案，能有效缓解置信度输出稀疏问题并提升置信度估计质量。

Abstract: Confidence estimation is essential when LLMs are used for classification, indicating when predictions can be trusted. However, common approaches such as verbalization produce extremely sparse outputs. For instance, Qwen3-32B verbalizes only eight unique confidence values on SST-2, with over half being exactly 95%, a pattern we observe consistently across four datasets and two LLMs. Besides limiting practical utility, we show that this sparsity critically affects evaluation: the choice of interpolation in area under the accuracy-rejection curve (AUARC) dramatically alters rankings, with consistency sampling dropping from best to worst under stepwise versus linear interpolation. We advocate for standardizing stepwise interpolation for a fairer comparison. Under such a fair evaluation, we find that weighting verbalized digits by token probabilities, a method we term verbalization logprobs, addresses sparsity and achieves the best AUARC (+2.3 points over vanilla verbalization) without incurring additional inference cost.

</details>


### [169] [Strengthening Target-Language Features: SAE-Based Steering for Multilingual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04904)
*Hongsheng Wang,Phlipp Koehn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）的推理时多语言引导方法，通过比较多语言平行句的SAE激活值，选择与目标语言相关的特征并将其解码为引导信号注入模型隐藏状态，无需额外训练即可提升多语言性能。在Gemma-3-12B-it上，XCOPA提升10.9个百分点，XNLI提升5.3个百分点，MGSM提升1.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大语言模型在不同语言间存在显著的性能差异，而现有的适应方法往往需要参数更新和大量多语言训练数据，成本高昂。作者希望在不进行训练的情况下，利用推理时的引导技术来提升低资源语言的性能。

Method: 使用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）识别并增强与目标语言相关的特征。具体步骤为：首先利用多语言平行句比较不同语言间的SAE激活值，选取少量与各目标语言相关的逐层特征；然后将这些特征解码为引导信号注入模型的隐藏状态，全程无需额外训练。

Result: 在Gemma-3-12B-it模型上，XCOPA平均准确率提升10.9个百分点，XNLI提升5.3个百分点，MGSM提升1.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 该推理时多语言引导方法通过SAE特征选择与解码实现训练无关的语言适应，在多个多语言基准上均取得了显著且一致的性能提升，展示了SAE特征在语言定向控制方面的潜力。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models exhibit substantial performance differences across languages, while existing adaptation methods often require parameter updates and considerable multilingual training data. We propose an inference-time multilingual steering method that uses pretrained sparse autoencoders to identify and strengthen target-language-related features. Using multilingual parallel sentences, we compare SAE activations across languages and select a small number of layer-specific features associated with each target language. These features are decoded into steering signals and injected into the model's hidden states without additional training. Experiments with Gemma-3-12B-it show average accuracy improvements of 10.9 percentage points on XCOPA, 5.3 points on XNLI, and 1.9 points on MGSM.

</details>


### [170] [Does Out-of-Sight Equal Out-of-Mind in CoT Monitorability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04928)
*Pedro Ferreira,Wilker Aziz,Ivan Titov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在从显式CoT切换到潜在CoT（latent CoT）后，模型推理的可监控性（monitorability）是否保持。通过基于提示线索的干预实验，作者发现可监控性更依赖于任务属性（如正确答案是否约束支撑推理）和内部状态访问级别，而非推理模式本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式CoT推理痕迹可被读取以监控模型的目标行为，但潜在CoT用少量连续状态替代显式token，降低了推理成本却移除了可读痕迹，使得监控需要依赖激活探测或将潜在状态转化为文本等替代手段，而这些手段能保留多少可监控性尚不明确。

Method: 采用基于提示线索（hint-based intervention）的实验设置，将提示依赖（hint-reliance）作为监控目标，在数学推理和问答任务上，对显式CoT、弱监督和强监督的潜在CoT等不同推理模式进行比较，评估不同监控器（monitor）的性能。

Result: 在提示线索干预设置下，可监控性更取决于任务属性（如正确答案是否约束支撑推理）以及模型内部状态访问的级别，而非推理模式本身。

Conclusion: 推理模式（显式CoT vs 潜在CoT）对可监控性的影响有限，任务性质和内部访问权限才是决定性因素，这对潜在CoT在安全与监控场景下的应用具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning offers a window into the decision-making of large language models (LLMs), which can be monitored for target behaviors by reading the reasoning trace, motivating work on CoT monitorability. Latent CoT approaches, however, replace the explicit tokens with a small number of continuous states, lowering inference costs but removing the readable trace this monitoring relies on. Monitoring then requires alternative access to the model, such as probing its activations or verbalizing the latent states back into text, but how much monitorability these alternatives preserve is unclear. We study this question with a hint-based intervention setup, a proxy for behaviors where models exploit biasing input cues, e.g., an inadvertently leaked answer or a belief stated by the user, without acknowledging them. Taking hint-reliance as the monitorability target, we compare monitors across reasoning modes, from explicit CoT to weakly- and strongly-supervised latent CoT, on math reasoning and question answering. We find that, in this setup, monitorability depends more on properties of the task (such as whether the correct answer constrains the supporting reasoning) and the level of access to model internals than on the reasoning mode.

</details>


### [171] [State2State: Environment-Derived Mid-Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04934)
*Xuanyu Lei,Yiqi Zhu,Chenliang Li,Kaiming Liu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye,Ya-Qin Zhang,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出State2State，一种无需外部任务定义和监督信号的智能体环境学习范式。它通过探索环境状态并将其转化为训练目标（让智能体到达指定目标状态），以规则匹配验证成功，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上验证了其作为环境学习阶段的独立效果，并可作为下游强化学习的初始化，提升最终性能和跨环境泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM智能体训练依赖于专家轨迹的监督微调或人工定义任务与验证器的在线强化学习，两者都受限于外部定义的任务和监督信号，限制了智能体训练的规模化和多样性。因此需要研究一种无需外部任务指定的环境学习范式，让智能体仅通过环境交互获得操控能力。

Method: State2State是一种环境推导的中期训练方法（environment-derived mid-training）。核心思路：首先通过环境探索收集智能体遇到的各类环境状态，将这些状态转换为训练目标（即挑战智能体到达指定的目标状态）；随后通过基于规则的原始状态匹配（rule-based state matching）来验证任务成功与否。整个过程无需专家轨迹、人工任务设计或外部验证器。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上的实验表明：State2State作为独立的环境学习阶段，在大多数设定下能提升智能体性能；作为下游强化学习的初始化时，能进一步提升最终性能和训练效率，并展现出有前景的跨环境泛化证据。

Conclusion: State2State证明从环境自身状态中可以自动推导出可扩展、可验证的训练目标，摆脱了对外部任务设计和大规模人工监督的依赖，为智能体环境学习提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: Training LLM agents commonly relies on supervised fine-tuning from expert trajectories or online reinforcement learning over human-specified tasks with handcrafted verifiers. Though effective, both remain bottlenecked by externally specified tasks and supervision signals, limiting the scalability and diversity of agent training. We study an environment learning paradigm in which agents acquire interaction and manipulation capabilities solely through environment interaction, without externally specified tasks. We propose State2State, an environment-derived mid-training method that converts explored environment states into training objectives, challenging agents to reach a specified target state. By deriving tasks from environment exploration and verifying success through rule-based state matching, State2State provides scalable and verifiable training objectives without expert supervision or manual task design. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that State2State improves agent performance as a standalone environment-learning stage in most settings. As initialization for downstream RL, it further improves final performance and learning efficiency, with promising evidence of cross-environment generalization.

</details>


### [172] [Reading Between the Frames: Interpreting Implicit and Non-literal Meaning in Social Media Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04939)
*Yang Wang,Yanan Ma,Yiqi Liu,Zi Yan Chang,Chi-Li Chen,Chia-Yi Hsiao,Tyler Loakman,Aline Villavicencio,Chenghao Xiao,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DrivelHub+基准，用于评估视频-语言模型能否推断社交媒体视频中隐含的、非线性的、修辞层叠的语用意义。基准包含1,000个来自社交媒体的视频，每个视频配有人工编写的隐含叙事解释，并通过解释和表征两个视角评估现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体视频的深层含义往往超越可见动作、字幕或语音，需要多模态线索与文化语境的交互才能理解，这构成了视频-语言模型难以应对的挑战性测试场景。

Method: 构建包含1,000个社交媒体视频的基准数据集，每个视频标注人工编写的隐含叙事解释；从两个视角评估：解释（模型需用自然语言解释视频的语用理解）和表征（将推理视为检索，测试视频-文本和文本-视频双向检索中模型表征是否对齐视频与隐含叙事）。

Result: 该基准提供了诊断性设置，用于衡量多模态感知与语用理解之间的差距，揭示了当前模型在从"描述所见"到"推断所指"之间的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前视频-语言模型在多模态感知上表现良好，但在推断社交媒体视频的隐含语用意义方面仍存在显著差距，DrivelHub+为该问题提供了系统化的诊断工具。

Abstract: Social media videos often communicate meanings that go beyond their visible actions, captions, or speech. A mundane clip may become humorous, ironic, or satire only through the interaction of multimodal cues and cultural context, making such content a difficult test case for video-language models. In this paper, we introduce \textit{DrivelHub+}, a benchmark for evaluating whether models can infer the implicit, non-linear, and rhetorically layered meanings of social media videos that appear nonsensical on the surface but convey deliberate pragmatic meanings. DrivelHub+ consists of 1,000 videos collected from social media, each annotated with a human-written implicit narrative explanation. Unlike conventional video understanding tasks focused on recognition or description, we present a benchmark that targets contextual multimodal reasoning. We evaluate current video-language models from two perspectives: explanation, where models must explain the pragmatic comprehension of a video in natural language; and representation, where we adapt reasoning-as-retrieval to test whether model representations align videos with their corresponding implicit narratives in both video-to-text and text-to-video retrieval. Our benchmark provides a diagnostic setting for measuring the gap between multimodal perception and pragmatic comprehension, asking whether current models can move beyond describing what is shown to inferring what is meant.

</details>


### [173] [Protoreasoning in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04980)
*Eduardo Valle,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"原型推理"(protoreasoning)——一种简单形式的思维链，可在约100万参数的小型transformer上实施，通过定义在Dyck语言上的推理友好任务，研究逐步推理的泛化机制。研究发现原型推理轨迹显著缩小了分布外泛化差距，且消融实验证实是轨迹内容而非额外token本身驱动了收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型展现出令人印象深刻的逐步推理能力，但人们尚未理解这种能力的普遍性，即LLM何时及如何学习到真正通用的算法而非"启发式集合"。这类问题难以在计算密集的前沿模型和不透明数据上研究，因此需要更小、更可控的模型来探索逐步推理的本质机制。

Method: 在远低于自然语言能力门槛的模型规模下（约100万参数的小型transformer），定义基于Dyck语言（正确嵌套括号的句子）的推理友好任务，采用一种简单形式的思维链——原型推理(protoreasoning)，并进行消融实验以区分轨迹内容与额外token各自的作用。

Result: 原型推理轨迹显著缩小了模型在分布外泛化上的差距；消融实验确认性能提升来源于轨迹的内容信息，而非仅仅是额外增加的token数量。

Conclusion: 小型transformer能够通过原型推理获得类似LLM逐步推理的泛化收益，为在极小模型上开展详细、可重复的推理机制实验提供了可行平台，有助于深入理解逐步推理的普遍性与算法学习机制。

Abstract: We show that tiny transformers can profitably employ a simple form of Chain of Thought, which we call protoreasoning, allowing us to study step-by-step reasoning on ~1M-parameter models and opening up opportunities for much more detailed experimentation and analysis than is feasible for larger models. Current Large Language Models exhibit impressive step-by-step reasoning, but we have yet to understand its generality, i.e., when and how LLMs learn genuinely general algorithms rather than "bags of heuristics." Such questions are hard to settle on compute-intensive frontier models trained on opaque data. To work at model scales far below the threshold for natural-language competence, we define reasoning-friendly tasks on Dyck languages (sentences of correctly nested brackets). We find that protoreasoning traces substantially close the out-of-distribution generalization gap, and ablations confirm that the trace's content, not merely its extra tokens, drives the gain.

</details>


### [174] [DelusionEval: Measuring Delusion-Linked Behaviors in AI Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05004)
*Jared Moore,Andrea Mock,Yifan Mai,Jacy Reese Anthis,Ryan Louie,William Agnew,Ashish Mehta,Kevin Klyman,Percy Liang,Nick Haber,Eric Lin,Desmond C. Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DelusionEval评估协议，用于测试大语言模型是否倾向于表现出与助长用户妄想相关的行为。基于18名经历过妄想和心理伤害用户的589段对话历史（共12,591条消息）进行测试，发现模型大小、发布日期、测试时推理能力与妄想关联行为无可靠相关，但扩展对话上下文会显著增加此类行为的发生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康专业人士担心LLM交互可能导致心理伤害风险（如"妄想螺旋"），随着LLM聊天机器人广泛使用，亟需基于真实世界用户心理伤害事件构建评估体系。

Method: 开发DelusionEval评估协议，用18名参与者的589段独特对话历史（12,591条消息）提示每个模型，测试模型表现出与助长用户妄想相关行为的倾向；系统性地改变上下文长度以测试其对行为率的影响。

Result: 模型大小、发布日期和测试时推理的存在与妄想关联行为无可靠相关；扩展上下文显著增加妄想关联行为率（如未劝阻自残率从30.0%升至41.1%）；所有模型家族（GPT、Claude等）均表现出实质性的妄想关联行为，且同家族内更新/更大/更强推理的模型并非在所有行为类别上一致更好。

Conclusion: LLM可能对用户造成心理伤害，现有评估需在真实世界人类-AI交互场景中更严格地研究，并强调上下文长度对LLM安全评估的重要性。

Abstract: Mental health professionals have raised concerns about risks of psychological harm from interaction with large language models (LLMs), including "delusional spirals" in which concerning human and LLM behaviors reinforce each other over time. With growing public use of LLM-powered chatbots, there is an urgent need to build evaluations grounded in real-world episodes of psychological harm experienced by users. We developed DelusionEval, an evaluation protocol that tests a model's tendencies to exhibit behaviors linked to promoting user delusions. We prompt each model with 589 unique conversation histories from 18 participants, comprising 12,591 messages from users who experienced delusions and psychological harm. We find that the tendency of an evaluated LLM to exhibit delusion-linked behavior does not reliably correlate with model size, release date, or the presence of test-time reasoning. However, extending the context of prior messages substantially increases rates of delusion-linked behaviors, providing evidence for the importance of context in LLM safety evaluation. For example, the rate of failing to discourage self-harm when the user expresses suicidal ideation increases from 30.0% to 41.1% when an additional 350 messages are prepended to the conversation history. All model families (e.g., GPT, Claude) exhibit substantial rates of delusion-linked behaviors. Within families, later, larger, or higher-reasoning models are not uniformly better across all behavior categories. Our results raise concerns regarding the potential psychological impact of LLMs and the need for more rigorous studies of real-world human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [175] [OneDayAgent: Towards a Long-Horizon Harness for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05013)
*Jingsheng Zheng,Xinyuan Fang,Jintian Zhang,Zhengke Gui,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OneDayAgent是一个面向开放域长时程任务的多智能体编排框架（harness），将开放式请求转化为受管理的执行过程：分解任务为有界子任务、在上下文压力下维护执行记忆、验证并修复最终交付物。在AgentIF-OneDay基准的104个任务上，使用GLM-5.2后端取得0.821的SOTA成绩，且同一harness可在三个模型家族的五个后端LLM上无需调优即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在处理开放式日常生活请求时面临长时程、跨环境、多模态的挑战，已有工作仅针对单个失败模式（如目标漂移、状态丢失、上下文溢出）进行修复，缺少研究一个统一的harness能否同时管理这些问题并在不同后端之间保持有效性。

Method: OneDayAgent将开放式请求转换为受管理的执行流程，具体包括：(1) 将任务分解为有界子任务（bounded subtasks）；(2) 在上下文压力下维护执行记忆（execution memory）；(3) 对最终交付物进行验证与修复（verify and repair）。

Result: 在AgentIF-OneDay基准的104个任务上，OneDayAgent配合GLM-5.2后端取得0.821的整体得分，达到新的SOTA。该harness无需调优即可在来自三个模型家族的五个后端LLM上运行，表明其具备跨后端的泛化能力，且不同模型在同一工作流下展现出不同的执行风格。

Conclusion: OneDayAgent证明了一个统一的智能体harness能够同时管理目标漂移、状态丢失和上下文溢出等多个失败模式，并在多个后端之间保持良好的泛化性，为长时程开放域智能体任务提供了一种可行的系统化解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to open-ended everyday requests that span work, study, and life. These tasks are long-horizon, cross-environment, and multimodal, forcing the agent to preserve goals and constraints across many steps while navigating heterogeneous tools and attachments. While prior work has addressed individual failure modes such as goals drift, states loss, and context overflow, whether a single harness can manage them jointly and remain effective across backends has received less study. We present OneDayAgent, a long-horizon harness for autonomous agents. OneDayAgent turns an open-ended request into a managed execution process that decomposes tasks into bounded subtasks, maintains execution memory under context pressure, and verifies and repairs the final deliverable. We evaluate OneDayAgent on AgentIF-OneDay across 104 tasks. With the GLM-5.2 backend, OneDayAgent sets a new state of the art with an overall score of 0.821. The same harness runs across five backend LLMs from three model families, indicating the harness generalizes across backends without tuning, even as different models induce distinct execution styles under the same workflow.

</details>


### [176] [Language Models Generalize to Human-like Word Order Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05028)
*Amanda Popadich,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在受控环境下训练语言模型，移除语料中所有含多个修饰语的名词短语，发现模型在三种规模下始终偏好与人类一致的作用域同态修饰语顺序，且发现PMI无法解释该偏好，表明语言模型能从贫乏输入中恢复类人语言概括。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言习得的一个核心问题是语言偏见能否从通用学习机制中涌现，而非依赖先天知识。人工语言学习（ALL）研究表明人类学习者倾向于作用域同态的修饰语排序，本文希望探究语言模型在类似条件下是否表现出相同偏见，从而为理解语言偏见的来源提供计算层面的证据。

Method: 作者创建了一个受控学习环境：从训练语料中删除所有含多个修饰语的名词短语，消除修饰语排序的直接证据；然后在三种模型规模上训练语言模型，并在包含多个修饰语的句子上进行评估；最后使用点互信息（PMI）分析名词-修饰语关联强度，以探察偏好的来源。

Result: 三种模型规模一致偏好作用域同态的修饰语顺序，尽管训练中从未观察到此类顺序；偏好强度因修饰语类型而异；PMI虽能反映已知的修饰语排序模式，但无法解释模型的排序偏好。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够从贫乏输入中恢复类人的语言概括（作用域同态修饰语排序），该框架为深入研究此类偏好的底层机制提供了受控实验条件。PMI并非这些偏好的直接来源，未来需探索其他可能的机制解释。

Abstract: A central question in language acquisition is whether linguistic biases can emerge from general learning mechanisms operating over underdetermined input. Artificial Language Learning (ALL) studies have shown that human learners reliably generalize beyond the evidence provided, including by preferring scope-homomorphic noun phrase modifier orders. In this work, we investigate whether language models exhibit the same bias under similar conditions. We create a controlled learning environment in which models are trained on a corpus where all noun phrases containing multiple modifiers have been removed, eliminating direct evidence about modifier ordering, and are then evaluated on multiple modifier sentences. Across three model sizes, we find that they consistently prefer scope-homomorphic orders despite never observing them during training. These preferences vary in strength by modifier type. To investigate the source of these preferences, we examine noun-modifier association strength using pointwise mutual information (PMI). While PMI reflects known modifier-ordering patterns, it does not explain the models' ordering preferences. These findings demonstrate that LMs can recover human-like linguistic generalizations from impoverished input and provide a controlled framework for investigating the mechanisms underlying such biases.

</details>


### [177] [Provable Limits and Certified Deferral for Verbalized Uncertainty in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05064)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究小型开放权重语言模型在私有、离线、低成本部署场景中，能否通过口头化置信度（verbalized confidence）支持风险可控的延迟决策（deferral）。基于三个模型家族、11个指令微调模型（0.5B至14B参数），在ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA上进行了25,168次本地预测，提出了三个理论结果界定校准的边界，并在实证中发现温度缩放不可行性下限，最终指出校准赋予置信度语义，而认证延迟决定了小模型何时可安全使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开放权重语言模型越来越多地部署在私有、离线且对成本敏感的场合，关键问题不仅是模型回答什么，还包括何时应该将决策延迟给人类。现有研究缺乏对口头化置信度能否支持风险可控延迟的系统性分析，本文旨在从理论和实证两个层面回答这一问题。

Method: （1）理论层面：提出三个理论结果——严格单调校准保持风险覆盖前沿与误差检测AUROC；温度缩放无法校准置信度始终高于1/2而准确率低于1/2的模型；Clopper-Pearson方法将200题校准集转化为有限样本风险认证。（2）实证层面：评估11个指令微调模型（0.5B-14B，三个家族）在ARC-Challenge和TruthfulQA上的表现，使用25,168个本地预测，检验温度缩放和Platt缩放等校准方法，并修复了TruthfulQA多选题中的答案顺序伪影。

Result: 22个模型-任务组合中有8个在温度缩放不可行性下限的一个百分点以内。Platt缩放将ECE降至最低0.02，但在20%风险预算下仅有3个模型-任务组合获得认证自主权，10%风险预算下无一获得。同时识别并修复了TruthfulQA多选题中的答案排序伪影。

Conclusion: 校准赋予置信度语义（calibration gives confidence semantics），但认证延迟（certified deferral）才决定小模型何时可安全使用。研究揭示了口头化置信度在支持风险可控延迟方面的理论边界和实证局限，为小型模型的安全部署提供了可操作的指导框架。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models increasingly run in private, offline, and cost-sensitive settings, where the key deployment question is not only what a model answers but when it should defer to a human. We study whether verbalized confidence can support risk-controlled deferral, evaluating eleven instruction-tuned models from three families, 0.5B to 14B parameters, on ARC-Challenge and TruthfulQA with 25,168 local predictions. Three theoretical results delimit what calibration can provide: strictly monotone calibration preserves the risk-coverage frontier and error-detection AUROC; temperature scaling cannot calibrate models whose confidence stays above one half while accuracy falls below it; and a Clopper-Pearson procedure converts a 200-question calibration set into a finite-sample risk certificate under an i.i.d. deployment assumption. Empirically, eight of 22 model-task pairs hit the temperature-scaling infeasibility floor within one percentage point of the predicted bound. Platt scaling reduces ECE to as low as 0.02, yet certified autonomy at a 20% risk budget is granted to only three model-task pairs and to none at 10%. We also identify and repair an answer-ordering artifact in the multiple-choice form of TruthfulQA. Calibration gives confidence semantics; certified deferral determines when small models are safe to use.

</details>


### [178] [German parties shifted towards intuition-based rhetoric after the far right's parliamentary breakthrough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05075)
*Peer Saleth,Segun T. Aroyehun,Fabio Carrella,Christoph M. Abels,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析2015-2025年间德国政治精英的450万条推文和59,170场议会演讲，发现直觉导向修辞日益突出，右翼行为者的"证据减直觉"（EMI）得分持续最低；极右翼AfD于2017年进入议会后，整个议会的EMI得分出现急剧下移，而Twitter上的下降更为渐进。这记录了极右翼可见性与精英话语中对待真相方式转变之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误信息的传播被视为对民主协商的威胁，但政治精英在真相承诺方面的修辞如何随民粹主义行为者的崛起而转变，目前仍缺乏深入理解。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，考察德国这一多党制欧洲民主国家中精英话语的变化。

Method: 使用经过验证的分布式词典表征方法来测量基于证据的修辞和基于直觉的修辞，分析2015-2025年间德国政治精英的4.5百万条推文和59,170场议会演讲数据，并计算证据减直觉（EMI）分数进行比较。

Result: 在议会和Twitter两个场域中，基于直觉的语言变得更加突出；右翼行为者的EMI分数始终最低。2017年极右翼AfD进入议会后，整个议会的EMI出现急剧下行变化，而Twitter上的下降则更为渐进。

Conclusion: 研究发现远右翼的可见性与精英话语中对待真相方式的转变之间存在关联，表明极右翼政党的进入可能改变了更广泛政治精英在真相修辞上的取向。

Abstract: The spread of misinformation is widely perceived as a threat to democratic deliberation, yet how political elites' rhetorical commitments to truth shift alongside the rise of populist actors remains poorly understood. Analysing 4.5 million tweets and 59,170 parliamentary speeches by German political elites between 2015 and 2025, we measure evidence-based and intuition-based rhetoric using a validated distributed dictionary representation. Across both arenas, intuition-based language has become more prominent, and right-leaning actors consistently exhibit the lowest Evidence Minus Intuition (EMI) scores. The parliamentary entry of the extreme-right Alternative for Germany (AfD) in 2017 coincides with sharp downward shifts in EMI across the broader chamber, while a more gradual decline is observed on Twitter. These findings document an association between far-right visibility and a changing approach to truth in elite discourse in a multiparty European democracy.

</details>


### [179] [Same Formulas, Different Semantics: Do Language Models Follow Modal Logic Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05097)
*Réemi Andrieu,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构造配对的模态逻辑问题（相同前提和猜想，但不同的框架/域条件），利用自动定理证明生成相反的标签，评估了语言模型是否遵循规定的模态语义。结果显示：在平衡核心集上，直接提示下五个最新模型中有四个低于仅基于条件推断的基线；但启用推理模式后，DeepSeek V4 Flash 在提示不变的情况下从 4.4% 跃升至 88.1%。模态语义的遵循高度依赖推理模式和模型身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态推理（必然性与可能性）依赖于世界之间的可达性关系以及每个世界中存在对象的假设，因此同一个推理在不同的模态系统中可能成立也可能不成立。评估语言模型在此类问题上的表现，需要测试其判断是否遵循给定的语义而非熟悉的逻辑，这是一个尚未被充分研究的领域。

Method: 构造配对的模态问题：每对问题具有相同的命题前提和猜想，但采用不同的框架条件（如传递性、自反性等）或域条件，通过自动推理工具验证其标签相反。同时设计平衡的核心测试集，使语义条件本身无法单独泄露答案。在五种最新模型上分别测试直接提示和推理模式（reasoning mode）下对规定模态语义的遵循程度。

Result: 在直接提示条件下，五个模型中四个的性能低于仅利用条件信息的基线；启用推理模式后，DeepSeek V4 Flash 的准确率从 4.4% 提升到 88.1%（提示文本未变）。当框架条件被省略时，各模型常常得出相同的结论，但各自拟合的熟悉逻辑体系各不相同。

Conclusion: 语言模型对规定模态语义的遵循既强烈依赖推理模式，也依赖具体模型的身份。研究还发布了公式、oracle 工件、反模型和模型响应等资源，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Reasoning about necessity and possibility depends on assumptions about accessibility between worlds and about which objects exist at each one. The same inference may therefore hold under one modal system and fail under another. Evaluating language models on such problems requires testing whether their judgments follow the stated semantics rather than a familiar logic. We construct paired modal problems with identical premises and conjecture but different frame or domain conditions; automated reasoning verifies opposite labels. A balanced core prevents the semantic condition alone from revealing the answer. On this core, four of five recent models perform below the condition-only baseline under direct prompting. Yet enabling reasoning mode raises DeepSeek V4 Flash from 4.4% to 88.1% on unchanged prompts. Following stipulated modal semantics thus depends strongly on inference mode as well as model identity. When frame conditions are omitted, models often agree but fit different familiar logics best. We release the formulas, oracle artifacts, countermodels, and responses.

</details>


### [180] [Chained Recursive Language Models for Multi-Iteration Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05124)
*Purbesh Mitra,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出链式递归语言模型（Chained RLM），一种推理时架构，通过多次以新的推理根（fresh reasoning roots）调用同一底层模型来完成长上下文推理任务。每次调用不继承完整对话历史，而是接收原始问题、上下文、紧凑的纯文本摘要、黑板（blackboard）和前任根生成的持久化任务特定构件（artifacts），从而将大推理任务切分为多个可检查、纠正和扩展的阶段计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理在LLM中通常受限，因为单次推理轨迹需要同时完成上下文探索、中间状态存储、证据验证和最终答案生成。在提取、计数、排序或多跳推理等任务中，早期错误会不断传播至最终答案，因此需要一种能将推理过程分解为可管理子任务并允许中间结果被检查和修正的架构。

Method: Chained RLM采用推理时架构：同一底层模型被反复调用为一连串新的推理根。每个根接收原始问题和上下文，但不继承完整对话历史；取而代之的是来自前任根的紧凑纯文本摘要、纯文本黑板和持久化的任务特定构件。该机制将长上下文管理转化为部分任务的顺序执行，每个阶段计算中的中间构件可由后续新的推理进行检查、纠正和扩展。论文描述了系统模型、交接机制、构件工作区和评估协议，并研究了新鲜上下文构件续接相对于直接LLM回答（即使递归工具调用）在准确性上的增益。

Result: 论文描述了Chained RLM的系统设计，并通过评估协议研究新鲜上下文构件续接在准确性上的可测增益。结果表明，在特定任务条件下，这种分阶段、带交接机制的推理方式相比直接LLM回答和递归工具调用具有可测量的准确性提升。

Conclusion: Chained RLM通过将长上下文推理分解为多个独立的推理阶段，利用紧凑摘要和构件交接机制管理上下文，避免早期错误在整个推理过程中的传播累积，为长上下文推理提供了一种有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Long context reasoning in large language models (LLMs) is usually constrained by the fact that a single inference trajectory has to simultaneously explore the context, store intermediate state, verify evidence, and produce the final answer. This becomes particularly difficult in tasks that require extraction, counting, ordering, or multi-hop reasoning, where an early mistake can propagate until the final response. In this work, we propose Chained Recursive Language Models (Chained RLM), an inference-time architecture, in which the same underlying model is called repeatedly as a sequence of fresh reasoning roots. Each root receives the original problem and context, but does not inherit the full conversational history. Instead, it receives a compact plain-text summary, a plain-text blackboard, and some durable task-specific artifacts written by predecessor roots. The motivation is to manage the context by chopping into partial tasks rather than one large inference response; in each staged computation, intermediate artifacts can be inspected, corrected, and extended by a later fresh inference by the same model. We describe the system model, handoff mechanism, artifact workspace, and evaluation protocol for this system. We study when fresh-context artifact continuation gives a measurable gain in accuracy over direct LLM answering even with recursive tool-calling.

</details>


### [181] [Spoken Function Calling: A New Perspective on Spoken Language Understanding for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05126)
*Yuezhang Peng,Yuxin Liu,Changfeng Gao,Zhifu Gao,Xiangang Li,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Spoken Function Calling (SFC)框架，将口语语言理解（SLU）从传统闭集任务范式扩展到开放域场景，通过结构化规则定义优化语义理解，并构建SFC-Bench数据集评估和增强LLM及LALM在语音函数调用方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLU在闭集任务上经过领域内监督微调后能有效提取用户语义，但在开放域任务中由于规则定义模糊，难以利用上下文学习（in-context learning）来泛化。这限制了SLU在开放域任务中的应用，亟需一种新的语义理解范式来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: （1）基于传统SLU数据集整理并扩展了一套口语函数（spoken functions）；（2）构建多智能体系统合成SFC-Bench数据集；（3）评估LLM和LALM在SFC任务上的性能；（4）通过后训练进一步提升LALM的SFC能力。

Result: 实验表明，SFC在语义提取准确性上显著优于传统SLU，能够大幅提升LLM和LALM的语义提取精度。

Conclusion: SFC作为一种新的语义理解视角，通过结构化规则定义使语义理解从传统闭集SLU范式进化到开放域场景，有效增强了LLM和LALM在语音函数调用上的表现。

Abstract: Spoken Language Understanding (SLU) is the core component of task-oriented dialogue systems and a pivotal link in achieving seamless human-agent interaction. While traditional SLU can effectively extract user semantics for closed-set tasks after in-domain supervised fine-tuning, it faces significant challenges in leveraging in-context learning for open-domain tasks due to its ambiguous rule definitions. This work proposes Spoken Function Calling (SFC), a novel semantic understanding perspective that optimizes semantic understanding with structured rule definitions, to evolve beyond traditional closed-set SLU. Specifically, we curate and extend a suite of spoken functions based on traditional SLU datasets, construct a multi-agent system to synthesize the SFC-Bench dataset, evaluate the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Audio Language Models (LALMs), and enhance the SFC capabilities of LALMs through post-training. Experiments demonstrate that SFC outperforms traditional SLU, substantially enhancing the semantic extraction accuracy for LLMs and LALMs.

</details>


### [182] [Toward Skill-Native LLMs: Skill Entropy for Benchmarking and Training Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05139)
*Yinghui He,Ling Yang,Jiarui Liu,Yongjin Yang,Lechen Zhang,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Mengdi Wang,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文定义了"跨技能长程推理"任务，提出Skill Entropy指标衡量技能切换难度，构建Skill^2-Bench基准（558个技能、9个领域），并设计Skill-Entropy RL训练框架，通过奖励技能序列与答案的联合正确性，在Qwen3系列模型上大幅提升基准得分（34.4%→68.4%；14.6%→40.1%），且可复用于现成训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准通常独立评估单一技能，缺乏对长推理链中"技能切换"能力的系统度量。作者希望从评测和训练两个维度填补这一空白，衡量并提升模型跨技能长程推理的能力。

Method: （1）提出Skill Entropy度量技能间切换难度；（2）构建Skill^2-Bench基准，涵盖558个技能、9个可验证开放领域，任务按技能熵分为三个难度级别；（3）提出Skill-Entropy RL框架：模型每步不仅预测答案，还预测所用技能；奖励=步骤级正确性 + 技能序列与金标准序列对齐的熵奖励。在Qwen3-4B和1.7B上验证，并迁移到OpenR1-Math等现成数据上。

Result: 对8个前沿闭源和4个开源模型评估发现"技能切换鸿沟"：高熵任务上准确率下降。Skill-Entropy RL在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上从34.4%提升至68.4%，在Qwen3-1.7B上从14.6%提升至40.1%，优于竞争基线；该流程可在OpenR1-Math等数据上复用。

Conclusion: 技能熵不仅可作为基准难度度量，还可转化为可复用的RL训练信号，显著提升小模型在跨技能长程推理任务上的表现，缩小与前沿模型的差距。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning in recent LLMs demands that the model switch between distinct skills inside a reasoning chain, such as first doing a math derivation, then using the result to plan a schedule. We call such problems cross-skill long-horizon tasks: multi-step tasks whose steps require different reasoning skills and depend on earlier outputs. Existing benchmarks often evaluate individual skills, lacking a principled way to measure how well a model switches between skills. We address this gap from both the evaluation and training sides. We introduce Skill Entropy, a measure of the difficulty of switching from one skill to another. We then propose Skill^2-Bench, a benchmark of cross-skill long-horizon tasks built over 558 skills across 9 verifiable and open-ended domains. Each task is assigned a task-level skill-entropy score and grouped into three difficulty levels. Evaluating 8 frontier and 4 open-source models on Skill^2-Bench reveals a skill-switching gap: accuracy decreases on higher-entropy tasks. We then turn skill entropy from a benchmark scale into a training signal. We propose Skill-Entropy RL, an RL framework where the model predicts not only the answer at each step but also the skill used to produce it. The reward combines step-level correctness with a skill-entropy reward that measures the alignment between the model-predicted skill sequence and the gold skill sequence. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct and Qwen3-1.7B, Skill-Entropy RL improves the Skill^2-Bench score from 34.4% to 68.4% and from 14.6% to 40.1%, respectively, outperforming competitive baselines. The same pipeline can be applied to off-the-shelf training data such as OpenR1-Math, indicating that skill entropy is a reusable training signal. Code available at: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/Skill-Entropy-RL

</details>


### [183] [Reasoning Core: Designing Broad Procedural Data for Completion-Supervised Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05148)
*Damien Sileo,Valentin Lacombe,Dimitri Kachler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Reasoning Core，一个包含50个程序化生成器的推理数据集合，覆盖数学、逻辑、规划等多个领域。通过匹配完整度监督协议下的对比实验，该系统在3B模型上于DROP、LogiQA、ARC-Challenge等多个基准中取得最高平均分，并系统审计揭示了生成、渲染、目标与评分之间的细微错配，强调程序化生成并不能保证正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成器虽然能以规模产生可验证的推理问题，但其作为完成度监督微调数据的潜力尚未被充分研究。作者希望系统性地构建并评估程序化推理数据在监督微调中的效用。

Method: 构建Reasoning Core集合（50个生成器，涵盖数学、逻辑、规划、状态跟踪、形式语言、结构化数据、游戏、因果与代码），配备语义评分器、难度控制和任务评估器；在匹配完成度监督协议下，与Procedural Warmup、Reasoning Gym和SynLogic在四种基础模型设置和多训练时长下对比；并结合模型辅助审查、人工裁决和回归测试进行审计。

Result: 在主要的3B模型对比中，Reasoning Core在DROP、LogiQA和ARC-Challenge上取得最高平均分，优于无程序化数据的基线和三种替代程序化数据集合；任务级分析表明语义有效性本身并不保证训练效用，紧凑目标和校准难度是关键设计因素；审计揭示了生成、渲染、目标和评分之间的细微错配。

Conclusion: 程序化生成本身不足以保证数据正确性，需要结合审计机制来发现和修正生成与标注之间的错配；紧凑目标和难度校准是提升程序化推理数据训练效用的重要设计因素。图书馆、生成数据集和审计材料已公开。

Abstract: Procedural generators produce useful verifiable reasoning problems at scale, but have received less attention as data for completion-supervised fine-tuning. We introduce Reasoning Core, a collection of 50 generators spanning mathematics, logic, planning, state tracking, formal languages, structured data, games, causality, and code, with semantic scorers, difficulty controls, and task evaluators. Under a matched completion-supervised protocol, we compare Reasoning Core with Procedural Warmup, Reasoning Gym, and SynLogic across four base-model settings and multiple training durations. In the primary 3B comparison, Reasoning Core achieves the highest mean scores on DROP, LogiQA, and ARC-Challenge, exceeding both the baseline without procedural data and all three alternative procedural collections. Task-level analyses show that semantic validity alone does not ensure training utility, highlighting compact targets and calibrated difficulty as important design factors. We ran audits combining model-assisted review, human adjudication, and regression testing. Applied throughout Reasoning Core development and to the other collections, they reveal subtle mismatches among generation, rendering, targets, and scoring, a reminder that procedural generation alone does not guarantee correctness. The library, generated datasets, and audit material are publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C²MOE框架，一个基于一致性和互补性引导的混合专家模型，用于解决多模态情感识别中模态缺失问题。该框架通过信息论原理将多模态知识分解为一致性与互补性成分，并采用双分支预测机制和可学习重加权模块实现鲁棒的模态补全，在多个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别在真实场景中常因传输错误或用户行为导致模态缺失，严重损害模型性能。现有方法主要依赖跨模态一致性学习来增强鲁棒性，但忽略了模态间的互补性信息，导致重建结果存在偏置。

Method: C²MOE采用信息论框架统一表示学习与模态缺失补全：(1) 通过交互感知专家将多模态知识分解为一致性（通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获）和互补性（通过最大化模态间条件熵保持）两个成分；(2) 引入双分支预测机制，一致性分支通过最小化不确定性将补全特征对齐到联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态独有线索；(3) 使用可学习重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性得分，实现自适应鲁棒融合。

Result: 在多个多模态情感识别基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C²MOE在各种模态缺失设置下均持续超越现有最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 同时考虑模态一致性与互补性对于不完整多模态情感学习至关重要，C²MOE通过信息论驱动的分解和自适应重加权机制有效解决了模态缺失下的补全偏置问题，为真实场景中的鲁棒多模态情感识别提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [185] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对亚优势（minmax）超度量算子的稳定性问题，发展了一套基于ℓ0范数的稳定性理论。核心发现是：稀疏扰动（编辑少量成对距离）的影响仅通过最小生成树（MST）传播，由此推导出每个编辑的暴露切割分数、仅依赖树的全局包络，以及Hamming-Lipschitz界，并证明了这些树几何依赖下界的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的超度量稳定性理论通常基于ℓ∞范数或Gromov–Hausdorff距离，这些界不适合稀疏扰动场景——即仅修改少数成对距离的情况。作者希望建立一套面向稀疏编辑的ℓ0型稳定性理论，以更精确地刻画单链接聚类/亚优势超度量算子在稀疏扰动下的行为。

Method: 1) 建立ℓ0型稳定性框架，分析稀疏编辑如何通过MST传播；2) 提出"暴露切割分数"（per-edit exposed-cut score）以衡量单个编辑的影响范围与深度；3) 构建"仅树全局包络"（tree-only global envelope）刻画整体变化上界；4) 证明Hamming-Lipschitz界，限制可改变的超度量条目数量；5) 构造反例证明树几何依赖的紧致性（严格切割分离下界恰好达到；单次离树编辑可使Θ(n²)条目变化）；6) 提出多编辑条件近可加性原理（在认证的大变化区域与可忽略聚合重叠下成立）。

Result: 1) 稀疏编辑的影响仅通过MST路径传播，成对超度量值仅在树路径交叉编辑边或离树编辑边暴露切割时改变；2) 获得每个编辑的尖锐暴露切割分数和仅依赖树的全局包络；3) 建立了Hamming-Lipschitz界；4) 证明了树几何依赖的紧致性：严格切割分离时树边界精确达到，离树编辑族中一次编辑可使Θ(n²)个超度量条目改变；5) 多编辑下条件近可加性原理成立；6) 在深度嵌入图谱实验上，结构分数可作为层次表示的漏洞诊断工具。

Conclusion: 建立的ℓ0型稳定性理论表明，亚优势超度量算子在稀疏扰动下的变化具有强烈的树结构依赖，影响传播受限于MST几何。这些理论结果不仅提供了尖锐的稳定性界，还为深层嵌入的层次表示提供了实用的漏洞诊断工具。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [186] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信任域框架来统一理解Adam等自适应矩估计优化器的行为，通过p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）控制每个权重的更新步长，推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制（Gmake）。实验表明在弱信任域约束下四阶矩（峰度）版本表现最佳，而强约束下二阶矩版本更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应矩估计机制（如Adam）在随机梯度优化中应用广泛，但其行为缺乏统一的理论解释框架。作者希望构建一个统一的信任域框架，将归一化、学习率调度、动量低通滤波和算子级谱归一化等现象纳入同一视角，从而更深入地理解这些机制的本质联系。

Method: 构建信任域框架，每个权重的更新步长大小受限于一个由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）支配的信任域。该推导产生一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制，其中p=4时涉及峰度类估计。整体机制命名为Gmake，在统一信任域框架下解释了多种优化技术。

Result: 在GPT2-124M（在FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories上训练）上的实验表明：四阶矩实现在信任域约束较弱时提供最大收益；随着引入更强的信任域控制，二阶矩实现变得更具竞争力，通常能达到略低于相应四阶矩实现的验证损失。

Conclusion: 信任域框架为理解自适应矩估计机制提供了统一视角。信任域约束的强弱是决定高阶矩（如四阶矩）是否优于二阶矩的关键因素：弱约束下高阶矩（峰度估计）更有优势，强约束下二阶矩（方差估计）更优。这一发现为实践中选择合适阶数的矩估计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [187] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用全卷积神经网络（FCN）自动检测中子透射光谱中的共振峰，以增强传统R-Matrix代码。尽管在七个光谱上达到约93%的分类准确率，但该指标高估了模型的泛化能力——即便加入更多训练数据，模型仍无法可靠泛化到未见过的同位素。未来工作需更大更丰富的数据集并结合物理先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R-Matrix代码拟合中子透射数据依赖先验评估且需要大量人工干预。中子透射数据复杂且有噪声，传统峰值识别方法难以有效分析，因此需要一种自动化的机器学习方法来加速实验后处理并减少对先验评估的依赖所引入的偏差。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对透射光谱中的每个点进行分类，判断其属于共振区域还是非共振区域。模型在七个透射光谱（两个评估数据、五个实验数据）上进行训练和测试，并在先前PHYSOR 2026分析的基础上增加了更多训练数据以验证泛化能力。

Result: 模型在分类任务上达到约93%的准确率。然而进一步分析表明该指标夸大了模型的实际泛化能力——即使额外增加了训练数据，模型仍无法在之前未见过的同位素上可靠地泛化。

Conclusion: 当前方法尚不足以作为可靠的共振检测工具投入使用。未来需要：(1) 评估更大且更多样化的训练数据集能否产生可泛化的模型；(2) 将中子共振的已知物理特征融入模型中以提高性能。该研究属于初步可行性研究，指出了问题的方向但尚未解决泛化这一核心挑战。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [188] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Lindblad量子开放系统原理启发的多时间尺度回声状态网络（Echo-State Network）储备池。该架构通过精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态构建循环算子，使旋转（控制相位混合）和衰减（控制记忆丢失）成为独立的设计变量，同时利用正交模态混合保持正态性，衰减谱直接决定回声状态稳定性裕度，无需后验谱半径缩放。在十个基准任务上与多种固定储备池和紧凑GRU对比，在NARMA-20上获得最佳固定储备池性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低均方误差，整体表现均衡且具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回声状态网络的储备池将信号混合、记忆保持和稳定性耦合在单一随机循环矩阵中，现有结构化设计虽然改进了拓扑、范数保持、泄漏或深度，但无法同时提供可逆混合与不可逆遗忘的独立模态控制，并缺乏直接的全局稳定性保证。

Method: 引入经典Lindblad启发的多时间尺度储备池，将循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态组装而成。旋转与衰减成为独立设计变量，分别控制相位混合与记忆丢失；正交模态混合保持矩阵正态性，衰减谱直接决定回声状态稳定性裕度，无需谱半径重新缩放。

Result: 在十个对齐种子上与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环和下一代储备池以及紧凑训练的GRU对比，在NARMA-20上取得最佳固定储备池性能，在Lorenz-63上获得最低平均误差，在线性记忆任务上与最强基线持平，整体表现广泛具有竞争力。消融研究表明旋转增加状态多样性，耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可解释的循环架构，其中混合、记忆和稳定性是显式且可独立调节的设计变量，弥合了开放系统动力学原理与结构化状态空间建模之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [189] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在开放世界学习(OWL)管道下游部署大语言模型(LLM)代理层，用于石油井异常检测。该代理不是替代上游方法，而是作为"伴侣"，将上游检测/分类/新颖性检测结果转换为人类可审计的自然语言解释、置信度排序的批判性反馈，以及为检测到的新颖性簇赋予可读名称。基于Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型（通过NVIDIA NIM服务），在3W数据集989个真实井文件段上实现35.1% top-1/63.9% top-3分类准确率、71.7% top-2验证准确率（精度0.91）、89.7%新颖性检测率（5/7隐藏类稳定命名）。核心目标是弥合OWL管道在运营部署中面临的可解释性鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWL管道虽然结合了自编码器检测、多类分类和马氏距离新颖性检测，能够回答"发生了什么"，但无法解释"模型为什么这样认为"或"操作员下一步该做什么"，并且无法为发现的新颖性簇赋予人类可读名称。这种可解释性缺失阻碍了OWL管道在实际运营场景中的部署。

Method: 在OWL管道下游部署基于Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型（通过NVIDIA NIM服务）的LLM代理层。代理接收结构化传感器指标和上游分类/新颖性断言，输出自然语言推理依据、按置信度排序的批判意见，以及整合后的新颖性命名。代理角色包括：(1)在传感器证据支持时确认上游决策；(2)以传感器可溯源的语言证明决策供操作员审计；(3)在上游标签不合理时标记分歧；(4)为新颖性簇命名。

Result: 在3W数据集的989个真实井文件段上进行三项研究：全九类分类达到35.1% top-1/63.9% top-3准确率（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]）；7个探测类达到71.7% top-2验证准确率（95% CI [64.8, 77.6]），精度0.91（95% CI [0.84, 0.95]）；新颖性检测达89.7%（95% CI [87.0, 91.9]），5/7隐藏类获得稳定簇命名。

Conclusion: 该LLM代理不是独立分类器，而是作为OWL管道的伴侣层，旨在弥合可解释性鸿沟，为操作员提供可审计的决策依据和可读的新颖性标签，从而推动OWL管道在运营环境中的实际部署。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [190] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个基于电阻式压力阵列的开放模型，仅通过压力数据即可回答文本查询。在STAG基准上（27个对象），top-1准确率达0.771±0.062，匹配甚至超过有监督CNN（0.76）且无需训练分类头。仅用187条训练录音、144k无标注帧的掩码自编码器预训练和传感器自身的校准仿射变换即可实现，同时详细报告了失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是最便宜且应用最广泛的触觉传感器，但现有触觉表征学习的研究几乎全部集中在光学传感器上（通过对形变凝胶成像）。作者希望填补这一空白，证明基于电阻式压力数据的表征学习同样可行。

Method: 1) 使用掩码自编码器（MAE）在144k无标注同传感器帧上进行预训练；2) 使用传感器自身的校准仿射变换进行数据预处理；3) 仅使用187条训练录音进行少样本训练；4) 使用记忆层（memory layer）进行开放文本查询匹配，无需训练分类头。

Result: STAG基准上top-1准确率0.771±0.062（四次运行），top-3达0.935，匹配并偶尔超过有监督CNN基线（0.76）。传感器校准仿射变换带来的精度提升超过所有架构改动之和；错误集中在少量接触模糊类别中，与文本目标几何无关（Spearman rho≤0.05）；两帧多样帧可恢复八帧精度的89%。同时也报告了失败：跨传感器预训练池化无增益、视觉协同训练损害触觉性能、错误归一化管道静默丢弃97%动态范围。

Conclusion: 作者证明基于低成本电阻式压力阵列的触觉表征学习可以达到与光学传感器方法相当的性能，且数据需求极小。文章强调虽然Tactus成功，但也诚实记录了失败的尝试，包括跨传感器预训练无效果和视觉协同训练的反效果，表明触觉表征学习需要专门针对压力数据特性的方法。模型权重、代码和记忆层均开源发布。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [191] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中针对航空发动机剩余寿命（RUL）预测模型的良性异质性和对抗性异质性两大挑战。通过多任务一维卷积神经网络和C-MAPSS基准的非IID划分，评估了四种良性异质性处理方法、五种攻击和四种聚合方法。结果显示：共享表征个性化可缩小约70%的精度差距；传感器值后门攻击在标准聚合下达到94.9%攻击成功率；Krum是唯一能抵御协同攻击的聚合器；个性化与鲁棒聚合结合可将攻击成功率降至2.8%且精度损失很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空机队运营商希望在不共享原始传感器数据的前提下，通过联邦学习联合训练剩余寿命预测模型。然而，机队间存在两种异质性挑战：良性异质性（不同运营商运行条件和故障模式不同）和对抗性异质性（被攻陷的运营商提交投毒更新）。准确率指标无法单独证明模型安全性，因此需要系统评估个性化方法和鲁棒聚合方法在安全性导向场景下的表现。

Method: 采用多任务一维卷积神经网络（MT-1DCNN），在C-MAPSS基准上构造结构性非IID划分。比较四种良性异质性处理方法（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务端重加权等），设计五种攻击（包括物理动机的传感器值后门攻击，用于掩盖发动机退化）针对四种聚合方法（标准平均、Krum等）进行评估。

Result: 共享表征个性化缩小了约70%的局部-集中式RMSE差距（近端正则化为21%，服务端重加权为10%）。传感器值后门攻击对标准平均聚合的攻成功率达94.9%，同时干净精度几乎不变。Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一能抵御协调攻击者的聚合器。个性化与鲁棒聚合结合后攻击成功率降至2.8%，仅带来少量精度损失。结果在客户端数量和更难的六条件数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 共享表征个性化是处理良性异质性的有效方法，但自身不提供针对投毒攻击的保护。必须将个性化与鲁棒聚合结合使用才能同时实现性能提升和安全性保障。准确率指标不足以认证模型安全性，攻击成功率必须作为显式评估指标。鲁棒更新选择与协作表征学习之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [192] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ)，一种后训练量化框架，通过低比特基权重加上一系列量化残差修正，从单一checkpoint支持多种有效精度（2/4/6/8-bit），无需校准数据，构建速度快（Qwen3-8B上比MatGPTQ快3.3倍），在6/8比特达到有竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化方法针对每种目标比特宽度需要单独的checkpoint，无法灵活适应多样化部署约束下对精度、内存占用和吞吐量的权衡需求。

Method: 从2-bit模型（通过PTQ或RTN获得）出发，使用RTN逐步添加轻量级2-bit残差来构建4/6/8-bit表示。该方法无需校准数据（calibration-free），避免联合多比特优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，完整的2/4/6/8-bit-RTN包在1,293秒内构建完成，比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。在六个最新LLM上的实验显示6和8比特下精度有竞争力，4比特下行为依模型而异。

Conclusion: RRQ实现了从单一checkpoint灵活获得多种精度模型的方案，在速度上显著优于现有方法，且在中高比特下精度有竞争力，代码将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [193] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMP是一个循环感知的多尺度Patch混合器，通过自适应循环学习模块按输入窗口识别主频率并生成历史与未来循环分量，通过地平线引导的Patch混合器实现位置相关细化，并通过多分辨率表示建模去循环残差。在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环感知预测器通常在数据集层面选择单一周期，难以应对周期随时间变化或多个周期并存的情况；Patch模型对所有patch位置进行统一处理，忽略了距离预测边界远近的差异；去循环后的残差动态跨越多个时间分辨率，单一尺度无法充分描述。

Method: CAMP包含三个关键模块：(1) 自适应循环学习模块，对每个输入窗口分别识别主导频率并生成历史和未来循环分量，无需预定义循环长度；(2) 地平线引导的Patch混合器，引入位置相关细化，较早的patch整合更广泛的时间上下文，同时保留靠近预测边界的信息；(3) 通过时间对齐的多分辨率表示对去循环残差建模，在单一框架内捕获不同尺度的互补动态。

Result: 在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在四个PEMS交通基准的十六个设置中取得最高MSE胜出次数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过窗口级别的自适应周期识别、位置感知的patch处理以及多尺度残差建模，有效解决了现有循环感知预测器在周期动态变化、多周期共存和多重时间分辨率下的局限性，在多个基准上展现了优越性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [194] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LaPrune是一种数学上精确预算的可微分层，通过LapSum障碍函数保持选择质量，并利用归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从稠密等质量分配推向硬top-k，实现可控稀疏且梯度可传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬top-k选择会阻断梯度，而连续松弛方法往往将掩码硬度与所选质量耦合，难以独立控制两者。

Method: 提出LaPrune，一种精确预算的可微分层，通过LapSum障碍函数保持选择质量，施加归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从稠密等质量分配推向硬top-k。推导了饱和分数的总体预测、近二元极限定律以及近零分数的紧最坏情况保证。

Result: 归一化硬度参数对分数尺度不变，而固定LapSum温度则不具备这一不变性。

Conclusion: LaPrune在保持选择质量的同时实现了可微分且可控硬度的稀疏选择，并且其归一化设计使其对分数尺度具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [195] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SJEPA是一个无重建的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）框架，通过将不透明的神经转移模型替换为"符号规律+正则化神经修正"的混合转移模型，学习具有简洁符号动力学描述的可预测表征，并形式化了"诱导动力学复杂度"原理以平衡预测保真度、表征质量、符号简洁性和符号-神经分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构能够从上下文嵌入预测目标嵌入来学习抽象状态，但其转移模型通常是无法解释的神经映射。这导致学到的表征缺乏可解释性和结构可控性，难以理解系统的底层动力学规律。作者希望让学习的预测表征具有简洁的符号描述，从而提升可解释性与可控性。

Method: SJEPA采用混合转移模型，由符号规律与正则化神经修正组成。框架通过两个核心机制实现"最简充分动力学"：(1) 表征约束保持信息丰富且不坍缩的可预测坐标；(2) 算子压缩偏好低复杂度的符号-神经转移。作者形式化了诱导动力学复杂度概念，分析了预测坐标的非可识别性，并证明了无约束算子压缩会导致表征坍缩的捷径。框架支持交替表征-方程学习和基于固定表征的符号动力学拟合两种模式。

Result: 在受控摆实验中，联合学习发现了显著更简洁的符号动力学，且长时程滚动误差和散度均低于事后拟合方法；无约束的单步诊断复现了预测的表征坍缩捷径。在文法错误设定下，修正正则化保留了可表征的符号机制，并将神经组件引导向残差动力学。

Conclusion: SJEPA揭示了预测保真度、表征质量、符号简洁性和符号-神经分配之间存在可控的权衡关系，为构建可解释、具有符号化动力学描述的预测表征学习框架提供了理论基础和实验验证。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [196] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA-KV，将KV缓存量化形式化为变换编码问题，推导出基于注意力感知失真准则的最优非正交变换，并结合等体积分组的等大小码本向量量化器，在每元素2比特下显著恢复长上下文检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码每步都需要读取KV缓存，加载开销超过注意力计算本身，导致解码吞吐受限于带宽。减少缓存大小可以同时提升解码速度和服务容量，但现有方法（如基于随机或Hadamard旋转的数据无关变换加固定宽度标量量化）在低比特率下无法有效压缩能量且量化次优。

Method: 将KV缓存量化建模为变换编码问题，以注意力乘积误差为失真准则。从校准统计量在高分辨率模型下推导出键和值的闭合形式最优变换。关键发现是最优键变换非正交，满足广义Parseval关系，使得注意力感知失真等价于变换域的MSE。因此可直接在变换后的键系数上使用MSE最优向量量化器。为满足固定宽度布局，通过等体积分组使等大小码本在高分辨率模型下达到可变码率最优。

Result: 在每元素2比特的压缩率下，NOVA-KV相比标量量化方法在可比吞吐量下恢复了大部分长上下文检索精度损失。

Conclusion: 通过从失真准则推导最优变换并结合适当的向量量化设计，NOVA-KV在极低比特率下显著优于现有标量量化方法，为带宽受限的长上下文解码提供了更优的KV缓存压缩方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [197] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于边缘计算流量预测的时空图Transformer框架，通过图神经网络捕获服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer自注意力学习长期时间依赖，在真实蜂窝网络数据集上优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中的流量预测对于主动资源管理至关重要，但现有循环预测方法虽能捕捉短期动态，却难以在非平稳条件下建模长期流量演化。需要在大规模时空场景中同时建模空间交互和长期时间依赖。

Method: 提出时空图Transformer框架，将空间表示学习与时间推理解耦：图神经网络用于捕获服务区域间的空间相关性，Transformer自注意力机制用于从历史流量观测中学习长期时间模式。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的图Transformer在多个预测时域上一致优于基于循环图网络的基线模型（GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU）。生成的预测支持更有效的主动资源调配，相比被动管理策略降低了过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强的注意力机制在构建智能自适应边缘计算系统中展现出显著潜力，能够更好地应对动态变化的服务需求，实现大规模时空流量建模与预测。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [198] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecDrop，一种无参数、固定权重的路由方案，用于探究MoE网络中路由性能瓶颈的来源。研究发现，当训练信号粒度与目标类别对齐时（如图像分类的单一超类标签），即使没有任何学习到的路由参数，路由仍能带来显著增益；而当训练信号粒度模糊（如语言建模）时，路由退化为No-Routing基线。结论是粒度对齐而非算法选择决定了路由的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE网络通过学习路由器、门控和负载均衡损失追求专家专业化，但在匹配总参数预算下，学习到的路由可能不如等权重的No-Routing基线。作者希望探究这一瓶颈究竟来自路由算法本身，还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐程度。

Method: SpecDrop是一种固定参数无关的路由方案：每个K分支对其分配类别获得权重p_a，对其他类别获得小的泄漏权重p_i>0，通过类别无关的固定分母合并，无学习路由参数、无辅助损失，推理时需要类别标签。在CIFAR-100/ResNet-110和ImageNet-1K/ViT-S/16上验证，并在SlimPajama-6B语言建模和SuperNI指令微调上进行模糊分区测试。

Result: 在CIFAR-100达到79.23%，ImageNet-1K达到79.89%，超越不使用标签的参数匹配基线（CIFAR-100上+4.75 vs dense，ImageNet-1K上+6.53 vs No-Routing+SE）。分支-类别对齐达58%/100%。在模糊分区上，路由机制在种子噪声范围内退化为No-Routing控制，验证了论文的核心假设。

Conclusion: 路由的性能瓶颈主要取决于训练信号粒度与目标类别的对齐程度，而非路由算法本身。类别监督通过路由部署能带来增益，但若将标签直接用于masking稠密模型输出可获得更高精度（85.2/83.7）；SpecDrop的核心价值在于将标签转化为训练好的模块化结构。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks pursue specialization through learned routers, gates, and load-balancing losses, yet at matched total-parameter budgets learned routers can underperform equal-weight No-Routing baselines. Is the bottleneck the routing algorithm, or the alignment between training-signal granularity and the target categories? We probe the question with SpecDrop, a fixed parameter-free routing scheme: each of $K$ branches receives weight $p_a$ for its assigned category and a small leakage $p_i > 0$ otherwise, merged through a category-independent fixed denominator, with no learned routing parameters and no auxiliary losses; the category label is required at inference. On vision tasks where each image has one superclass label (CIFAR-100 on ResNet-110; ImageNet-1K on ViT-S/16), SpecDrop reaches 79.23% on CIFAR-100 and 79.89% on ImageNet-1K, exceeding parameter-matched baselines that do not use the label (+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100; +6.53 over the No-Routing+SE control on ImageNet-1K). These gains quantify what category supervision buys when deployed through routing -- not an advantage over label-aware deployments of the baselines: given the same label, masking a dense model's outputs is stronger for accuracy alone (85.2 / 83.7). SpecDrop's contribution is converting the label into trained-in modular structure: 58%/100% branch-category alignment, and masking gains of 0.00 (CIFAR) / +1.06 (ImageNet) -- the output-space restriction is largely internalized during training. On fuzzy partitions, where training units span multiple categories (SlimPajama-6B language modeling with a 30M Transformer; SuperNI instruction tuning over Llama-3.2-1B with LoRA), the routing mechanism reduces to the matched No-Routing controls within seed noise, the null our thesis predicts. Granularity alignment, not algorithm choice, localizes when routing helps. Code: https://github.com/Beryex/SpecDrop

</details>


### [199] [Out-Of-The-Loop Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04113)
*Gustavo Sutter,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准多保真贝叶斯优化（MF-BO）算法在真实场景中的次优性问题——当最高保真度函数过于昂贵无法进入优化循环时，算法性能会退化。作者提出一种通过整合历史高保真度数据及其任务描述符（可显式给出或从非结构化元数据中提取）来缓解该问题的方法，并在合成函数、化学和超参数优化等真实问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际MF-BO任务中，真实的最高保真度目标函数过于昂贵，无法直接纳入优化循环；但实践中通常存在来自先前实验的最高保真度"黄金标准"数据（例如化学家通过计算机模拟选出top-k候选分子后揭示其真实目标值），这些历史数据可能为当前任务提供有价值的信息。标准MF-BO算法未利用这些数据，导致即使在理想假设下也表现次优。

Method: 作者首先通过理论分析或实验论证标准MF-BO算法在上述真实场景中的次优性；然后提出一种方法，将历史高保真度数据及其任务描述符（task descriptors）整合进优化框架中——任务描述符可以显式给出，也可以从非结构化元数据中提取，以跨任务传递信息，弥补最高保真度函数缺失带来的性能损失。

Result: 在合成函数、化学任务以及超参数优化等真实问题上，所提出的方法均表现出有效性，验证了整合历史高保真度数据与任务描述符能够显著改善MF-BO在最高保真度函数不可用时的优化性能。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了标准MF-BO在最高保真度函数不可参与优化循环时的局限性，并提出了利用历史黄金标准数据及任务描述符的解决方案，为实际应用中昂贵高保真度评估下的多保真优化提供了更实用的方法论。

Abstract: Black-box optimization is a ubiquitous problem in science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions with cheaper lower-fidelity proxies available. Multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MF-BO) is a principled approach to this problem, leveraging correlations across different fidelities when querying the objective. However, for many important MF-BO tasks, the true highest-fidelity function is prohibitively expensive to be part of the optimization loop. Nevertheless, practitioners often have gold standard data (observations of the highest-fidelity function) obtained from previous experiments that might provide information for the current task. For instance, in molecular optimization, chemists often pick the top-$k$ candidate molecules using various computer simulations, and later reveal their true objective function values. In this work, we demonstrate the suboptimality of standard MF-BO algorithms in the real-world scenarios above, even under ideal assumptions. Next, we mitigate this problem by incorporating historical high-fidelity data accompanied by task descriptors---which can be explicitly given or extracted from unstructured metadata. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on synthetic functions, as well as real-world problems in chemistry and hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [200] [LiNC: Lightweight Noise Correction via Per-Sample Trust and Gaussian Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04147)
*Abhishek Moturu,Babak Taati,Anna Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LiNC方法，通过为每个训练样本引入单一可学习的信任参数，在标准训练循环中学习何时使用观测标签、何时依赖模型预测，以应对医学影像数据集中的标签噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
